<p><span class="pn">01</span> Except as provided in Paragraph 3 of this Section, if increased fines are imposed for traffic violations within a designated zone of a roadway, a BEGIN HIGHER FINES ZONE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-10.svg">R2-10</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-3">Figure 2B-3</a>) or a FINES HIGHER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-6p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-6P.svg">R2-6P</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2b-3">Figure 2B-3</a>) shall be used to provide notice to road users.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If an <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-10.svg">R2-10</a> sign or an <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-6p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-6P.svg">R2-6P</a> plaque is posted to provide notice of increased fines for traffic violations, an END HIGHER FINES ZONE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-11.svg">R2-11</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-3">Figure 2B-3</a>) shall be installed at the downstream end of the zone to provide notice to road users of the termination of the increased fines zone.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The BEGIN HIGHER FINES ZONE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-10.svg">R2-10</a>) sign or FINES HIGHER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-6p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-6P.svg">R2-6P</a>) plaque may be omitted where the higher fines zone is established by statute.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The BEGIN HIGHER FINES ZONE sign or FINES HIGHER plaque should be located at the beginning of the temporary traffic control zone, school zone, or other applicable designated zone and just beyond any interchanges, major intersections, or other major traffic generators.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> Agencies should limit the use of the Higher Fines signs and plaque to locations where work is actually underway, or to locations where the roadway, shoulder, or other conditions, including the presence of a school zone and/or a reduced school speed limit zone, require a speed reduction or extra caution on the part of the road user.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> The Higher Fines signs and plaque shall have a black legend and border on a white rectangular background. All supplemental plaques mounted below the Higher Fines signs and plaque shall have a black legend and border on a white rectangular background.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The FINES HIGHER plaque shall be mounted below an applicable regulatory or warning sign in a temporary traffic control zone (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/6g/#6g08">Section 6G.08</a>), a school zone (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/7b/#7b06">Section 7B.06</a>), or other applicable designated zone.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> Alternate legends such as BEGIN (or END) DOUBLE FINES ZONE may also be used for the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-10.svg">R2-10</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-11.svg">R2-11</a> signs.</p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> The legend FINES HIGHER on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-6p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-6P.svg">R2-6P</a> plaque may be replaced by FINES DOUBLE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-6ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-6aP.svg">R2-6aP</a>), $XX FINE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-6bp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-6bP.svg">R2-6bP</a>), or another legend appropriate to the specific regulation (see <a href="#fig-2b-3">Figure 2B-3</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> The following may be mounted below an <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-10.svg">R2-10</a> sign or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-6p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-6P.svg">R2-6P</a> plaque: A. A supplemental plaque specifying the times that the higher fines are in effect (similar to the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/s4-1p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/S4-1P.svg">S4-1P</a> plaque shown in <a href="#fig-7b-1">Figure 7B-1</a>), B. A supplemental plaque WHEN CHILDREN (WORKERS) ARE PRESENT, or C. A supplemental plaque WHEN FLASHING (similar to the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/s4-4p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/S4-4P.svg">S4-4P</a> plaque shown in <a href="#fig-7b-1">Figure 7B-1</a>) if used in conjunction with a Speed Limit Sign Beacon (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/4s/#4s04">Section 4S.04</a>).</p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Movement And Lane Control Signs And Plaques</div>


<span id="2b26" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.26 Movement Prohibition Signs (R3-1 through R3-4, R3-18, and R3-27)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.26 &middot; manual page 89 &middot; <span class="badge rev">Revision 1 page</span> &middot; <a class="badge ca-amend" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b26">CA amended: 2B.26</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.26/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Movement Prohibition signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) shall be installed where specific movements are prohibited at an intersection approach except as provided in Paragraphs 13 and 17 of this Section.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Movement Prohibition signs should only be used to prohibit a turn or through movement from an entire approach and should not be used to designate movements that are required or permitted from a specific lane or lanes on a multi-lane approach.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Movement Prohibition signs should be placed where they will be most easily seen by road users who might be intending to make the movement.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If a No Right Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-1.svg">R3-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) is used, at least one should be placed either over the roadway or at a right-hand corner of the intersection.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> If a No Left Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-2.svg">R3-2</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) is used, at least one should be placed over the roadway, at the far left corner of the intersection, on a median, or in conjunction with the STOP sign or YIELD sign located on the near right corner.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Except as provided in Item C in Paragraph 11 of this Section for signalized locations, if a NO TURNS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-3.svg">R3-3</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) is used, two signs should be used, one at a location specified for a No Right Turn sign and one at a location specified for a No Left Turn sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> If a No U-Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-4.svg">R3-4</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) or a combination No U or Left Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-18/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-18.svg">R3-18</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) is used, at least one should be used at a location specified for a No Left Turn sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> If both left turns and U-turns are prohibited, the combination No U or Left Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-18/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-18.svg">R3-18</a>) sign should be used instead of separate <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-2.svg">R3-2</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-4.svg">R3-4</a> signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2b/#2b27">Sections 2B.27</a> through 2B.30 contain information regarding lane control signs that indicate the required or permitted movements from individual lanes.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> If a No Straight Through (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-27/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-27.svg">R3-27</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) is used, at least one should be placed either over the roadway or at a location where it can be seen by road users who might be intending to travel straight through the intersection.</p>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> If turn prohibition signs are installed in conjunction with traffic control signals: A. The No Right Turn sign should be installed adjacent to a signal face viewed by road users in the right-hand lane. B. The No Left Turn (or No U-Turn or combination No U or Left Turn) sign should be installed adjacent to a signal face viewed by road users in the left-hand lane. C. A NO TURNS sign should be placed adjacent to a signal face viewed by all road users on that approach, or two signs should be used.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> If turn prohibition signs are installed in conjunction with traffic control signals, an additional turn prohibition sign may be post-mounted to supplement the sign mounted overhead.</p>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> Where ONE WAY signs are used (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2b/#2b49">Section 2B.49</a>), No Left Turn and No Right Turn signs may be omitted.</p>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> Where the movement restriction applies to certain vehicle classes, signs incorporating a supplementary legend, modified as appropriate, may be used to indicate the specific vehicle class restriction (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-1b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-1b.svg">R3-1b</a> through <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-1d/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-1d.svg">R3-1d</a>) or exception (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-1e/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-1e.svg">R3-1e</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-1f/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-1f.svg">R3-1f</a>) (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>). When the movement restriction applies during certain time periods only, the following Movement Prohibition signing alternatives may be used and are listed in order of preference: A. A blank-out or changeable message sign (see Chapter 2L) that displays the prohibited movement only during the time that the movement prohibition is applicable, especially at signalized intersections. B. Permanently-mounted signs incorporating a supplementary legend showing the hours and days during which the prohibition is applicable (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-1g/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-1g.svg">R3-1g</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-1h/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-1h__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-1h</a>) (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>). C. Portable signs, installed by proper authority, located off the roadway at each corner of the intersection. The portable signs are only to be used during the time that the movement prohibition is applicable.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">15</span> The blank-out part-time electronic-display Movement Prohibition sign shall consist of a red circle and diagonal with a white prohibited movement on an opaque black background.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">16</span> Movement Prohibition signs may be omitted at a ramp entrance to an expressway or a channelized intersection where the design is such as to indicate clearly the one-way traffic movement on the ramp or turning lane.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">17</span> The No Left Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-2.svg">R3-2</a>) sign, the No U-Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-4.svg">R3-4</a>) sign, and the combination No U or Left Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-18/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-18.svg">R3-18</a>) sign shall not be used at approaches to roundabouts to prohibit drivers from turning left onto the circulatory roadway of a roundabout.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">18</span> At roundabouts, the use of <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-2.svg">R3-2</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-4.svg">R3-4</a>, or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-18/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-18.svg">R3-18</a> signs to prohibit left turns onto the circulatory roadway might confuse drivers about the possible legal turning movements around the roundabout. Roundabout Circulation (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-5p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-5P.svg">R6-5P</a>) plaques (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2b/#2b51">Section 2B.51</a>) and/or ONE WAY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-1__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R6-1</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-2.svg">R6-2</a>) signs are appropriate to indicate the travel direction within a roundabout.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-grid"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-4"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0090.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0090.webp" alt="Figure 2B-4. Movement Prohibition and Lane Control Signs and Plaques"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-4. Movement Prohibition and Lane Control Signs and Plaques <span class="src-page">p 90</span></figcaption></figure></div>

<span id="2b27" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.27 Intersection Lane Control Signs (R3-5 through R3-8)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.27 &middot; manual page 91 &middot; <span class="badge rev">Revision 1 page</span> &middot; <a class="badge ca-amend" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b27">CA amended: 2B.27</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.27/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Intersection Lane Control signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>), if used, shall require road users in certain lanes to turn, shall permit turns from a lane where such turns would otherwise not be permitted, shall require a road user to stay in the same lane and proceed straight through an intersection, or shall indicate permitted movements from a lane.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Intersection Lane Control signs have three applications: A. Mandatory Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5.svg">R3-5</a> series and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a> series) signs, B. Optional Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-6__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-6</a> series) signs, and C. Advance Intersection Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> series) signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> When Intersection Lane Control signs are mounted overhead, each sign used should be placed over the lane or a projection of the lane to which it applies.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> On signalized approaches where through lanes that become mandatory turn lanes, multiple-lane turns that include shared lanes for through and turning movements, or other lane-use regulations are present that would be unexpected by unfamiliar road users, overhead Intersection Lane Control signs should be installed at the signalized location over the appropriate lanes or projections thereof and in advance of the intersection over the appropriate lanes.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> Where overhead mounting on the approach is impracticable for the Advance and/or Intersection Lane Control signs, one of the following alternatives should be employed: A. At locations where through lanes become mandatory turn lanes, a Mandatory Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a>) sign should be post-mounted on the left-hand side of the roadway where a through lane is becoming a mandatory left-turn lane on a one-way street or where a median of sufficient width for the signs is available, or on the right-hand side of the roadway where a through lane is becoming a mandatory right-turn lane. B. At locations where a through lane is becoming a mandatory left-turn lane on a two-way street where a median of sufficient width for the signs is not available, and at locations where multiple-lane turns that include shared lanes for through and turning movements are present, an Advance Intersection Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> series) sign should be post-mounted in a prominent location in advance of the intersection, and consideration should be given to the use of an oversized version in accordance with <a href="#fig-2b-1">Table 2B-1</a>.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Use of an overhead sign for one approach lane should not require installation of overhead signs for the other lanes of that approach.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> Intersection Lane Control signs may be omitted where: A. A turn bay has been provided by physical construction or pavement markings, and B. Only the road users using such turn bays are permitted to make a turn in that direction.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> At roundabouts, Intersection Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5.svg">R3-5</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-6__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-6</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> series) signs may display any of the arrow symbol options shown in <a href="#fig-2b-5">Figure 2B-5</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-grid"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-4"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0091.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0091.webp" alt="Figure 2B-4. Movement Prohibition and Lane Control Signs and Plaques"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-4. Movement Prohibition and Lane Control Signs and Plaques <span class="src-page">p 91</span></figcaption></figure></div>

<span id="2b28" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.28 Mandatory Movement Lane Control Signs (R3-5, R3-5a, R3-7, R3-19 Series, and R3-20) and Plaques


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.28 &middot; manual page 92 &middot; <span class="badge rev">Revision 1 page</span> &middot; <a class="badge ca-amend" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b28">CA amended: 2B.28</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.28/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Mandatory Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5.svg">R3-5</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5a.svg">R3-5a</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>), if used, shall indicate only the single vehicle movement that is required from the lane.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The Mandatory Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5.svg">R3-5</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5a.svg">R3-5a</a>) symbol signs shall include the legend ONLY and shall be mounted overhead over the specific lanes to which they apply (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2b/#2b27">Section 2B.27</a>). The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a> sign shall be for post-mounting only. The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a> sign shall not be mounted at the far side of the intersection.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> When the mandatory movement applies to lanes exclusively designated for HOV traffic, the HOV 2+ (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5cp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5cP.svg">R3-5cP</a>) supplemental plaque shall be used. When the mandatory movement applies to lanes that are not HOV facilities, but are lanes exclusively designated for buses and/or taxis, the TAXI LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5dp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5dP.svg">R3-5dP</a>) and/ or BUS LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5gp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5gP.svg">R3-5gP</a>) supplemental plaques shall be used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If used, the Mandatory Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a>) sign shall be located in advance of the intersection, such as near the upstream end of the mandatory movement lane, and/or at the near side of the intersection where the regulation applies.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The use of the Mandatory Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a>) word message sign should be limited to only locations that are adjacent to the full-width portion of a mandatory turn lane. The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a> sign should not be installed adjacent to a through lane in advance of a turn bay taper or adjacent to a turn bay taper.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Mandatory Movement Lane Control signs should be accompanied by lane-use arrow markings, especially where traffic volumes are high, where there is a high percentage of commercial vehicles, or where other distractions exist.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> Where the restriction does not apply to buses or bicycles an EXCEPT BUSES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7aP.svg">R3-7aP</a>) or EXCEPT BICYCLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7bp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7bP.svg">R3-7bP</a>) plaque should be used.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> The Through Only (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5a.svg">R3-5a</a>) sign may be used to require a road user in a particular lane to proceed straight through an intersection.</p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> The diamond symbol may be used instead of the word message HOV on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5cp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5cP.svg">R3-5cP</a> supplemental plaque.</p>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> Where a mandatory left or U-turn lane is added at a median location, a LANE FOR LEFT TURN ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-19/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-19.svg">R3-19</a>) or LANE FOR U TURN ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-19a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-19a.svg">R3-19a</a>) sign may be post-mounted on the median at the beginning of the taper. Where a U turn and a left turn are both allowed, a LANE FOR U AND LEFT TURNS ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-19b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-19b.svg">R3-19b</a>) sign may be used. Where a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-19/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-19.svg">R3-19</a> series sign is used, Mandatory Movement Lane Control signs along the turn lane in the median may be omitted.</p>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-19/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-19.svg">R3-19</a> series signs may be used where the added median turn lane is separated from the through lanes by a channelizing or divisional island.</p>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> On an approach to a mandatory turn lane where traffic regularly enters the shoulder to access the turn lane inappropriately, creating safety or operational issues, a DO NOT DRIVE ON SHOULDER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-17/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-17.svg">R4-17</a>) sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2b/#2b43">Section 2B.43</a>) may be used to supplement the standard Mandatory Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5.svg">R3-5</a> and/or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a> series) signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-grid"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-5"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0092.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0092.webp" alt="Figure 2B-5. Intersection Lane Control Sign Arrow Options for Roundabouts"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-5. Intersection Lane Control Sign Arrow Options for Roundabouts <span class="src-page">p 92</span></figcaption></figure></div>

<span id="2b29" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.29 Optional Movement Lane Control Signs (R3-6 Series)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.29 &middot; manual page 93 &middot; <span class="badge rev">Revision 1 page</span> &middot; <a class="badge ca-amend" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b29">CA amended: 2B.29</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.29/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Optional Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-6__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-6</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-6a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-6a.svg">R3-6a</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-6b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-6b.svg">R3-6b</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>), if used, shall be used for two or more movements from a specific lane or to emphasize permitted movements. The Optional Movement Lane Control sign shall be mounted overhead over the specific lane to which it applies.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If used, the Optional Movement Lane Control signs shall indicate all permissible movements from specific lanes.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Because more than one movement is permitted from the lane, the word message ONLY shall not be used on an Optional Movement Lane Control sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Optional Movement Lane Control signs shall be used for two or more movements from a specific lane where a movement, not allowed by State statute or local ordinance, is permitted.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The Optional Movement Lane Control signs shall not be used alone to effect a turn prohibition.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> If used, the Optional Movement Lane Control sign should be located overhead in advance of the intersection, such as near the upstream end of an adjacent mandatory movement lane, and/or overhead at the intersection where the regulation applies.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b30" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.30 Advance Intersection Lane Control Signs (R3-8 Series)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.30 &middot; manual page 93 &middot; <span class="badge rev">Revision 1 page</span> &middot; <a class="badge ca-amend" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b30">CA amended: 2B.30</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.30/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Advance Intersection Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> Series) signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) may be used to indicate the configuration of all lanes ahead.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The word messages ONLY, THRU, HOV 2+, TAXI, BUS, or BIKE, or the bicycle symbol, may be used within the border in combination with the arrow symbols of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> sign series. The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5cp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5cP.svg">R3-5cP</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5dp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5dP.svg">R3-5dP</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5gp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5gP.svg">R3-5gP</a> supplemental plaques may be installed at the top outside border of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> sign over the applicable lane designation on the sign. The diamond symbol may be used instead of the word message HOV. The minimum allowable vehicle occupancy requirement may vary based on the level established for a particular facility.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Where a bicycle lane is between two general-purpose lanes the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> series signs may be modified to show the bicycle lane with a white legend on a black background in accordance with designs of the R3-8x series signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> When used, an Advance Intersection Lane Control sign should be placed at an adequate distance in advance of the intersection, either along the lane tapers or at the beginning of the turn lane so that road users can select the appropriate lane (see <a href="#fig-2a-4">Figure 2A-4</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> An Advance Intersection Lane Control sign may be repeated closer to the intersection along the approach for additional emphasis.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> An Advance Intersection Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> series) sign shall not be mounted at the far side of an intersection to which it applies.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> Where three or more approach lanes are available to traffic, Advance Intersection Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> series) signs, if used, shall be post-mounted in advance of the intersection and shall not be mounted overhead.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> When only the two outermost lanes of the roadway are shown on a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> sign, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5bp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5bP.svg">R3-5bP</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5fp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5fP.svg">R3-5fP</a> plaque shall be mounted above the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> sign.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b31" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.31 RIGHT (LEFT) LANE MUST EXIT Signs (R3-33, R3-33a)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.31 &middot; manual page 94 &middot; <a class="badge ca-amend" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b31">CA amended: 2B.31</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.31/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A RIGHT (LEFT) LANE MUST EXIT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-33/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-33__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-33</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) may be used to supplement an overhead EXIT ONLY guide sign to inform road users that traffic in the right-hand (left-hand) lane of a roadway that is approaching a grade-separated interchange is required to depart the roadway on the exit ramp at the next interchange.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-33a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-33a__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-33a</a> sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) may be used in place of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-33/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-33__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-33</a> sign where the roadside width is limited and will not accommodate the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-33/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-33__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-33</a> sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2c/#2c50">Section 2C.50</a> contains information regarding a warning sign that can be used in advance of lane drops at grade-separated interchanges.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b32" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.32 Two-Way Left-Turn-Only Signs (R3-9a and R3-9b) and Plaques


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.32 &middot; manual page 94 &middot; <a class="badge ca-amend" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b32">CA amended: 2B.32</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.32/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A Two-Way Left-Turn-Only (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9a.svg">R3-9a</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9b.svg">R3-9b</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-6">Figure 2B-6</a>) should be used in conjunction with the required pavement markings where a non-reversible lane is reserved for the exclusive use of left-turning vehicles in either direction and is not used for passing, overtaking, or through travel.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The post-mounted <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9b.svg">R3-9b</a> sign may be used as an alternate to or a supplement to the overhead <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9a.svg">R3-9a</a> sign. The legend BEGIN or END may be used within the border of the main sign itself, or on an <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9cp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9cP.svg">R3-9cP</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9dp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9dP.svg">R3-9dP</a> plaque (see <a href="#fig-2b-6">Figure 2B-6</a>) mounted immediately above it.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Signing is especially helpful to drivers in areas where the two-way left-turn-only maneuver is new, in areas subject to environmental conditions that frequently obscure the pavement markings, and on peripheral streets with two-way left-turn-only lanes leading to an extensive system of routes with two-way left-turn-only lanes.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b33" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.33 BEGIN and END Plaques (R3-9cP and R3-9dP)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.33 &middot; manual page 94 &middot; <a class="badge ca-same" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b33">CA MUTCD 2026: 2B.33</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.33/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The BEGIN (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9cp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9cP.svg">R3-9cP</a>) or END (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9dp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9dP.svg">R3-9dP</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2b-6">Figure 2B-6</a>), mounted directly above a regulatory sign, may be used to inform road users of the location where a regulatory condition begins or ends.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b34" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.34 Reversible Lane Control Signs (R3-9e through R3-9i)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.34 &middot; manual page 94 &middot; <a class="badge ca-same" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b34">CA MUTCD 2026: 2B.34</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.34/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A reversible lane may be used for through traffic (with left turns either permitted or prohibited) in alternating directions during different periods of the day, and the lane may be used for exclusive left turns in one or both directions during other periods of the day as well. Reversible Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9e/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9e.svg">R3-9e</a> through <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9i/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9i.svg">R3-9i</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-6">Figure 2B-6</a>) may be either static type or changeable message type. These signs may be either post-mounted or overhead. Lane-use control signals (see Chapter 4T) may also be used for reversible lanes.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Where it is determined by an engineering study that lane-use control signals or physical barriers are not necessary, the lane shall be controlled by overhead Reversible Lane Control signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-7">Figure 2B-7</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Post-mounted Reversible Lane Control signs shall be used only as a supplement to overhead signs or signals. Post-mounted signs shall be identical in design to the overhead signs and an additional legend such as CENTER LANE shall be added to the top of the sign (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9f/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9f.svg">R3-9f</a>) to indicate which lane is controlled.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Reversing traffic flow may be controlled with pavement markings and Reversible Lane Control signs (without the use of lane-use control signals), when all of the following conditions are met: A. Only one lane is being reversed, B. An engineering study indicates that the use of Reversible Lane Control signs alone would result in an acceptable level of safety and efficiency, and C. There are no unusual or complex operations in the reversible lane pattern.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> Reversible Lane Control signs shall contain the legend or symbols designating the allowable uses of the lane and the time periods such uses are allowed. Where symbols and legends are used, their meanings shall be as shown in <a href="#fig-2b-2">Table 2B-2</a>.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Reversible Lane Control signs shall consist of a white background with a black legend and border, except for the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9e/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9e.svg">R3-9e</a> sign, where the color red is used for the X symbol.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> Symbol signs, such as the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9e/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9e.svg">R3-9e</a> sign, shall consist of the appropriate symbol in the upper portion of the sign with the appropriate times of the day and days of the week below it. All times of the day and days of the week shall be accounted for on the sign to eliminate confusion to the road user.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> In situations where more than one message is conveyed to the road user, such as on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9e/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9e.svg">R3-9e</a> sign, the sign legend shall be arranged as follows: A. The prohibition or restriction message is the primary legend and shall be on the top for word message signs and to the far left for symbol signs, B. The permissive use message shall be displayed as the second legend, and C. The OTHER TIMES message shall be displayed at the bottom for word message signs and to the far right for symbol signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> The symbol signs may also include a downward-pointing arrow with the legend THIS LANE. The term OTHER TIMES may be used for either the symbol or word message sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> A Reversible Lane Control sign shall be mounted over the approximate center of the lane that is being reversed.</p>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> If the vertical or horizontal alignment is curved to the degree that a driver would be unable to see at least one sign, and preferably two signs, then additional overhead signs shall be installed. The placement of the signs shall be such that the driver will have a definite indication of the lanes specifically reserved for use at any given time. Special consideration shall be given to major generators introducing traffic between the normal sign placement.</p>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> Transitions at the entry to and exit from a section of roadway with reversible lanes shall include advance signs to notify or warn drivers of the boundaries of the reversible lane controls. The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9g/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9g__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-9g</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9h/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9h__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-9h</a> signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-6">Figure 2B-6</a>) shall be used for this purpose.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> More than one End Reverse Lane (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9i/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9i.svg">R3-9i</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-6">Figure 2B-6</a>) may be used at the termination of the reversible lane to emphasize the importance of the message.</p>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> Where longitudinal barriers separate opposing directions of traffic, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9g/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9g__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-9g</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9h/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9h__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-9h</a> signs may be omitted.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">15</span> Flashing beacons, if used to supplement the overhead Reversible Lane Control signs, shall comply with the applicable requirements for flashing beacons in Chapter 4S.</p>
<p><span class="pn">16</span> When used in conjunction with Reversible Lane Control signs, the Turn Prohibition (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-1.svg">R3-1</a> through <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-4.svg">R3-4</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-18/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-18.svg">R3-18</a>) signs shall be mounted overhead and separate from the Reversible Lane Control signs. The Turn Prohibition signs shall be designed and installed in accordance with <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2b/#2b26">Section 2B.26</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">17</span> For additional emphasis, a supplemental plaque stating the distance of the prohibition, such as NEXT 1 MILE, should be added to the Turn Prohibition signs that are used in conjunction with Reversible Lane Control signs.</p>
<p><span class="pn">18</span> If used, overhead signs should be located at intervals not greater than ¼ mile. The bottom of the overhead Reversible Lane Control signs should not be more than 19 feet above the pavement grade.</p>
<p><span class="pn">19</span> Where more than one sign is used at the termination of a reversible lane, they should be at least 250 feet apart. Longer distances between signs are appropriate for streets with speeds over 35 mph, but the separation should not exceed 1,000 feet.</p>
<p><span class="pn">20</span> Because left-turning vehicles have a significant impact on the safety and efficiency of a reversible lane operation, if a mandatory left-turn lane or two-way left-turn lane cannot be incorporated into the lane-use pattern for a particular peak or off-peak period, consideration should be given to prohibiting left turns and U-turns during that time period.</p>
<p><span class="pn">21</span> Reversible Lane Control signs and parking signs should be consistent in message during the same operational periods.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-grid"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-6"></span><span id="fig-2b-2"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0095.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0095.webp" alt="Figure 2B-6. Center and Reversible Lane Control Signs and Plaques; Table 2B-2. Meanings of Symbols and"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-6. Center and Reversible Lane Control Signs and Plaques; Table 2B-2. Meanings of Symbols and <span class="src-page">p 95</span></figcaption></figure>
<figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-7"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0096.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0096.webp" alt="Figure 2B-7. Location of Reversible Two-Way Left-Turn Signs"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-7. Location of Reversible Two-Way Left-Turn Signs <span class="src-page">p 96</span></figcaption></figure></div>

<span id="2b35" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.35 Jughandle Signs (R3-23, R3-24, R3-25, and R3-26 Series)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.35 &middot; manual page 97 &middot; <a class="badge ca-same" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b35">CA MUTCD 2026: 2B.35</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.35/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A jughandle turn is a left turn or U-turn that because of special geometry is made by initially making a right turn. This type of turn can increase the operational efficiency of a roadway by eliminating the need for mandatory left-turn lanes and can increase the operational efficiency of a traffic control signal by eliminating the need for protected left-turn phases. A jughandle turn can also provide an opportunity for trucks and commercial vehicles to make a U-turn where the median and roadway are not of sufficient width to accommodate a traditional U-turn by these vehicles.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> <a href="#fig-2b-8">Figure 2B-8</a> shows the various signs that can be used for signing jughandle turns. <a href="#fig-2b-9">Figure 2B-9</a> shows examples of regulatory and destination guide signing for various types of jughandle turns.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> On multi-lane roadways, since road users generally anticipate that they need to be in the left-hand lane when approaching a location where they desire to turn left or make a U-turn, an ALL TURNS FROM RIGHT LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-23/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-23.svg">R3-23</a>) or a U TURN FROM RIGHT LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-23a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-23a.svg">R3-23a</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-8">Figure 2B-8</a>) shall be installed in advance of the location to inform drivers that left turns and/or U-turns will be made from the right-hand lane.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Where a median of sufficient width is available, supplemental regulatory or guide signs may also be placed on the left-hand side of the roadway.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-24/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-24.svg">R3-24</a> series sign with an upward diagonal arrow pointing to the right if the jughandle entrance is designed as an exit ramp (see Drawings A and B in <a href="#fig-2b-9">Figure 2B-9</a>), or the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-25/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-25.svg">R3-25</a> series sign with a horizontal arrow pointing to the right if the jughandle entrance is designed as an intersection, shall be installed on the right-hand side of the roadway at the entrance to the jughandle. The legend on the sign shall be ALL TURNS, U TURN, or U AND LEFT TURNS, as appropriate.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> If the jughandle is designed such that the jughandle entrance is downstream of the location where the turn would normally have been made (see Drawing C in <a href="#fig-2b-9">Figure 2B-9</a>), the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-26/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-26.svg">R3-26</a> series sign with an arrow pointing straight upward shall be installed on the right-hand side of the roadway at the intersection to inform road users that they need to proceed straight through the intersection in order to make a left turn or U-turn. The legend on the sign shall be U TURN or U AND LEFT TURNS, as appropriate.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-24/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-24.svg">R3-24</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-25/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-25.svg">R3-25</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-26/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-26.svg">R3-26</a> series of signs are designed to be mounted below conventional guide signs.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2c/#2c12">Section 2C.12</a> contains information regarding the use of advisory exit and ramp speed signs for exit ramps.</p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2d/#2d40">Section 2D.40</a> contains information regarding the use of guide signs for jughandles.</p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Passing, Keep Right, And Slow Traffic Signs</div>

<div class="mfig-grid"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-8"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0098.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0098.webp" alt="Figure 2B-8. Jughandle Regulatory Signs"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-8. Jughandle Regulatory Signs <span class="src-page">p 98</span></figcaption></figure>
<figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-9"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0099.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0099.webp" alt="Figure 2B-9. Examples of Applications of Jughandle Regulatory and Guide Signing"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-9. Examples of Applications of Jughandle Regulatory and Guide Signing <span class="src-page">p 99</span></figcaption></figure>
<figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-9"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0100.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0100.webp" alt="Figure 2B-9. Examples of Applications of Jughandle Regulatory and Guide Signing"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-9. Examples of Applications of Jughandle Regulatory and Guide Signing <span class="src-page">p 100</span></figcaption></figure>
<figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-9"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0101.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0101.webp" alt="Figure 2B-9. Examples of Applications of Jughandle Regulatory and Guide Signing"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-9. Examples of Applications of Jughandle Regulatory and Guide Signing <span class="src-page">p 101</span></figcaption></figure></div>

<span id="2b36" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.36 DO NOT PASS Sign (R4-1)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.36 &middot; manual page 102 &middot; <a class="badge ca-amend" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b36">CA amended: 2B.36</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.36/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Do Not Pass (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-1.svg">R4-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be used in addition to pavement markings (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/3b/#3b03">Section 3B.03</a>) to emphasize the restriction on passing. The Do Not Pass sign may be used at the beginning of, and at intervals within, a zone through which sight distance is restricted or where other conditions make overtaking and passing inappropriate.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If signing is needed on the left-hand side of the roadway for additional emphasis, NO PASSING ZONE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-3.svg">W14-3</a>) signs may be used (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2c/#2c53">Section 2C.53</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Standards for determining the location and extent of no-passing zone pavement markings are set forth in <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/3b/#3b03">Section 3B.03</a>.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b37" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.37 PASS WITH CARE Sign (R4-2)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.37 &middot; manual page 102 &middot; <a class="badge ca-same" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b37">CA MUTCD 2026: 2B.37</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.37/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The PASS WITH CARE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-2.svg">R4-2</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) should be installed at the downstream end of a no- passing zone if a Do Not Pass sign has been installed at the upstream end of the zone.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b38" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.38 KEEP RIGHT EXCEPT TO PASS Sign (R4-16), SLOWER TRAFFIC KEEP RIGHT Sign (R4-3), and TRUCKS USE RIGHT LANE Sign (R4-5)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.38 &middot; manual page 102 &middot; <a class="badge ca-amend" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b38">CA amended: 2B.38</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.38/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The KEEP RIGHT EXCEPT TO PASS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-16/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-16.svg">R4-16</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be used on roadways where there are two lanes in one direction of travel to direct drivers to stay in the right-hand lane except when they are passing another vehicle.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If used, the KEEP RIGHT EXCEPT TO PASS sign should be installed at or just beyond the beginning of a two-lane section of roadway and at selected locations along two-lane roadways where additional emphasis is needed.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The SLOWER TRAFFIC KEEP RIGHT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-3.svg">R4-3</a>) or the TRUCKS USE RIGHT LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-5.svg">R4-5</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be used on multi-lane through roadways to improve capacity or reduce unnecessary lane changing due to the presence of slower vehicles that impede the normal flow of traffic.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If used, the SLOWER TRAFFIC KEEP RIGHT sign or the TRUCKS USE RIGHT LANE sign should be installed at or just beyond the beginning of a multi-lane roadway section or at the beginning of an extra lane provided for trucks and/or other slow-moving traffic, and at selected locations where there is a tendency on the part of some road users to drive in the left-hand lane(or lanes) below the normal speed of traffic. These signs should not be used on the approach to an interchange or through an interchange area where traffic is entering or exiting, or along deceleration or acceleration lanes.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The TRUCKS USE RIGHT LANE sign may be used as a supplement to the SLOWER TRAFFIC KEEP RIGHT sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> If an extra lane has been provided for trucks and other slow-moving traffic, a Lane Ends sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2c/#2c47">Section 2C.47</a>) should be installed in advance of the point where the extra lane ends. Appropriate pavement markings should be installed at both the upstream and downstream ends of the extra lane (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/3b/#3b12">Section 3B.12</a> and <a href="#fig-3b-14">Figure 3B-14</a>).</p>
</div>

<span id="2b39" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.39 Keep Right and Keep Left Signs (R4-7 Series and R4-8 Series)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.39 &middot; manual page 102 &middot; <a class="badge ca-amend" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b39">CA amended: 2B.39</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.39/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Keep Right (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7.svg">R4-7</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be used at locations where it is necessary for traffic to pass only to the right-hand side of a roadway feature or obstruction. The Keep Left (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-8.svg">R4-8</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be used at locations where it is necessary for traffic to pass only to the left-hand side of a roadway feature or obstruction.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> At locations where it is not readily apparent that traffic is required to keep to the right, a Keep Right sign should be used.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> If Keep Right signs are installed at the start of a median or at a median opening, they shall be placed as close as practicable to the approach ends of the medians, and shall be visible to traffic on the divided highway and angled toward the applicable crossroad approach as shown in <a href="#fig-2b-20">Figure 2B-20</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If used, the Keep Right sign should be mounted on the face of or just in front of a pier or other obstruction separating opposite directions of traffic in the center of the highway such that traffic will have to pass to the right-hand side of the sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> Where the approach end of the island channelizes traffic away from the approach direction, the word legend (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7a.svg">R4-7a</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7b.svg">R4-7b</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-8a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-8a.svg">R4-8a</a>, or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-8b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-8b.svg">R4-8b</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) should be used instead of the symbol (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7.svg">R4-7</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-8.svg">R4-8</a>) signs to emphasize the degree of curvature away from the approach direction (see <a href="#fig-2b-11">Figure 2B-11</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Where a regulatory sign is used within the central island of a neighborhood traffic circle to direct traffic counter-clockwise around the central island, the Keep Right with diagonal arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7b.svg">R4-7b</a>) sign should be used (see <a href="#fig-2b-24">Figure 2B-24</a>). The mounting height of the sign should be at least 4 feet, measured vertically from the bottom of the sign to the elevation of the near edge of the traveled way.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The Keep Right (Left) sign shall not be installed on the right-hand (left-hand) side of the roadway in a position where traffic must pass to the left-hand (right-hand) side of the sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> The Keep Right sign may be omitted at intermediate ends of divisional islands and medians.</p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> Word message KEEP RIGHT (LEFT) with an arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7a.svg">R4-7a</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7b.svg">R4-7b</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be used instead of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7.svg">R4-7</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-8.svg">R4-8</a> symbol signs.</p>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> A narrow Keep Right (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7c/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7c.svg">R4-7c</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be installed on the approach end of a median island that is less than 4 feet wide at the point where the sign is to be located.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> A narrow Keep Right (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7c/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7c.svg">R4-7c</a>) sign shall not be installed on a median island that has a width of 4 feet or more at the point where the sign is to be located.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> The Keep Right sign may be installed in the median of a divided highway crossing that functions as a single intersection such that it is visible to traffic on the divided highway and angled as needed toward the applicable crossroad approach as shown in <a href="#fig-2b-20">Figure 2B-20</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2b/#2b49">Section 2B.49</a> provides more information about the use of the Keep Right sign in combination with or in lieu of ONE-WAY signs at divided highway crossings.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-grid"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-10"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0103.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0103.webp" alt="Figure 2B-10. Passing, Keep Right, and Slow Traffic Signs"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-10. Passing, Keep Right, and Slow Traffic Signs <span class="src-page">p 103</span></figcaption></figure></div>

<span id="2b40" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.40 STAY IN LANE Sign (R4-9)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.40 &middot; manual page 104 &middot; <a class="badge ca-same" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b40">CA MUTCD 2026: 2B.40</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.40/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A STAY IN LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-9/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-9.svg">R4-9</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be used on multi-lane highways to direct road users to stay in their lane until conditions permit shifting to another lane.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If a STAY IN LANE sign is used, it should be accompanied by a solid double white lane line(s) to prohibit lane changing.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b41" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.41 RUNAWAY VEHICLES ONLY Sign (R4-10)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.41 &middot; manual page 104 &middot; <a class="badge ca-amend" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b41">CA amended: 2B.41</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.41/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A RUNAWAY VEHICLES ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-10.svg">R4-10</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) should be installed near a truck escape (or runaway truck) ramp entrance to discourage other road users from entering the ramp.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-grid"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-11"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0105.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/mutcd/p0105.webp" alt="Figure 2B-11. Examples of Keep Right and Keep Left Sign Placement"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-11. Examples of Keep Right and Keep Left Sign Placement <span class="src-page">p 105</span></figcaption></figure></div>

<span id="2b42" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.42 Slow Vehicle Turn-Out Signs (R4-12, R4-13, and R4-14)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.42 &middot; manual page 106 &middot; <a class="badge ca-amend" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b42">CA amended: 2B.42</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.42/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> On two-lane highways in areas where traffic volumes and/or vertical or horizontal curvature make passing difficult, turn-out areas are sometimes provided for the purpose of giving a group of faster vehicles an opportunity to pass a slow-moving vehicle.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> A SLOW VEHICLES WITH XX OR MORE FOLLOWING VEHICLES MUST USE TURN-OUT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-12.svg">R4-12</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be installed in advance of a turn-out area to inform drivers who are driving so slow that they have accumulated a specific number of vehicles behind them that they are required by the traffic laws of that State to use the turn-out to allow the vehicles following them to pass.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The specific number of vehicles displayed on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-12.svg">R4-12</a> sign provides law enforcement personnel with the information they need to enforce this regulation.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If an <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-12.svg">R4-12</a> sign has been installed in advance of a turn-out area, a SLOW VEHICLES MUST USE TURN- OUT AHEAD (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-13/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-13.svg">R4-13</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may also be installed downstream from the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-12.svg">R4-12</a> sign, but upstream from the turn-out area, to remind slow drivers that they are required to use a turn-out that is a short distance ahead.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> If an <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-12.svg">R4-12</a> sign has been installed in advance of a turn-out area, a SLOW VEHICLES MUST TURN OUT (with arrow) (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-14/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-14.svg">R4-14</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) shall be installed at the entry point of the turn-out area.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2d/#2d54">Section 2D.54</a> contains information regarding advance information signs for slow vehicle turn-out areas.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b43" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.43 DO NOT DRIVE ON SHOULDER Sign (R4-17) and DO NOT PASS ON SHOULDER Sign (R4-18)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.43 &middot; manual page 106 &middot; <a class="badge ca-same" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b43">CA MUTCD 2026: 2B.43</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.43/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The DO NOT DRIVE ON SHOULDER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-17/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-17.svg">R4-17</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be installed to inform road users that using the shoulder of a roadway as a travel lane is prohibited.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The DO NOT PASS ON SHOULDER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-18/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-18.svg">R4-18</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be installed to inform road users that using the shoulder of a roadway to pass other vehicles is prohibited.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b44" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.44 ALL TRAFFIC Sign (R4-20) and RIGHT (LEFT) TURN ONLY Sign (R4-21)


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.44 &middot; manual page 106 &middot; <a class="badge ca-same" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b44">CA MUTCD 2026: 2B.44</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.44/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The ALL TRAFFIC (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-20/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-20.svg">R4-20</a>) sign may be used at an intersection where all traffic on the approach to the intersection must turn in the direction indicated and the Movement Prohibition (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2b/#2b26">Section 2B.26</a>) and/or ONE WAY (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2b/#2b49">Section 2B.49</a>) signs do not adequately convey the allowable direction of travel.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The RIGHT (LEFT) TURN ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-21/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-21__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R4-21</a>) sign may be used at or on an approach to an intersection where all traffic on that approach must turn in the direction indicated.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The RIGHT (LEFT) TURN ONLY sign should not be used for a channelized turn lane separated from the adjacent travel lanes by an island.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The ALL TRAFFIC sign shall not be used to substitute for the Keep Right (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7.svg">R4-7</a> series) or Keep Left (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-8.svg">R4-8</a> series) signs.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The RIGHT (LEFT) TURN ONLY sign shall not be used to substitute for the Mandatory Movement Lane Control signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2b/#2b27">Sections 2B.27</a> and 2B.28).</p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Selective Exclusion Signs And Plaques</div>


<span id="2b45" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.45 Selective Exclusion Signs and Plaques


<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.45 &middot; manual page 107 &middot; <a class="badge ca-amend" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b45">CA amended: 2B.45</a> &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/us/2B.45/">short link</a></div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Selective Exclusion signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-12">Figure 2B-12</a>) may be used to provide notice to road users that State or local statutes or ordinances exclude designated types of traffic from using particular roadways or facilities.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Selective Exclusion signs shall clearly indicate the type of traffic that is excluded.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Typical exclusion messages include: A. No Trucks (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-2.svg">R5-2</a>), B. NO MOTOR VEHICLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-3.svg">R5-3</a>), C. NO COMMERCIAL VEHICLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-4.svg">R5-4</a>), D. NO VEHICLES WITH LUGS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-5.svg">R5-5</a>), E. No Bicycles (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-6.svg">R5-6</a>), F. NO NON-MOTORIZED TRAFFIC (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-7.svg">R5-7</a>), G. NO MOTOR-DRIVEN CYCLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-8.svg">R5-8</a>), H. No Pedestrian Crossing (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-3.svg">R9-3</a>), I. No Skaters (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-13/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-13.svg">R9-13</a>), J. No Equestrians (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-14/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-14.svg">R9-14</a>), K. No Snowmobiles (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-15.svg">R9-15</a>), L. No All-Terrain Vehicles (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-16/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-16.svg">R9-16</a>), M. Hazardous Material (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r14-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R14-3.svg">R14-3</a>) (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/mutcd/2b/#2b67">Section 2B.67</a>), N. NO THRU TRAFFIC (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-12.svg">R5-12</a>), O. NO THRU TRUCKS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-2b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-2b.svg">R5-2b</a>), P.
